## `AFG-4311-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Seismograph-Vibration Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Controlled Demolition Blasting Vibration Seismograph Monitoring
- **Intro:** Seismograph-Vibration Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Controlled Demolition Blasting Vibration Seismograph Monitoring — Pre-Calibration Safety and Environmental Check: Ambient temperature: 0°C to 40°C; relative humidity: ≤85%; geophone orientation: ±2° from true north; exclusion radius: ≥300 m.
- **Key tolerance:** Ambient temperature: 0°C to 40°C; relative humidity: ≤85%; geophone orientation: ±2° from true north; exclusion radius: ≥300 m.
- **Standards:** `ISO 4866:2010`, `ANSI/ASA S2.47-2013 (R2018) Guidelines for the measurement of vi`, `Afghanistan National Standards Authority (ANSA)`, `ISO 8041:2005`
